Every originated market, rung by rung, with its basis

1,622 originated prices across 67 fixtures. Every price is a zero-vig fair price derived from the model probability printed beside it; the 5% book-equivalent is shown in grey for comparison only. Each market prints the basis it was built from, including which values are sourced and which are labelled modelling assumptions. Rungs outside 5%–95% are dropped so no degenerate price is published. Totals exist only for the sports where both clubs' scoring rates were read on an official page.
